+/**
+ * A TRANSIENT backend error that genai.mil / Gemini returns as a normal 200 "answer" ("internal error in code
+ * generation, you can try again later") must be detected + silently RE-RUN, not shown as the final reply. And the
+ * detector must NOT match a long, legitimate answer that merely discusses a code-generation error.
+ * Run: node test/test-backend-transient-error.mjs
+ */
+import { isTransientBackendError } from '../src/agent/worker-guards.mjs';
+import { runMinimalWorker } from '../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+function mockAdapter(script) { let i = 0; return { async complete() { const c = script[Math.min(i++, script.length - 1)]; return { choices: [{ message: { content: c } }], usage: { total_tokens: 5 } }; } }; }
+async function run(dir, script) {
+  const events = [];
+  const result = await runMinimalWorker({
+    adapter: mockAdapter(script), model: 'test', signal: { aborted: false },
+    messages: [{ content: 'what happens during registration?' }], projectDir: dir,
+    requireBranch: () => 'ok', requireApproval: () => true,
+    onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {},
+  });
+  result.events = events;
+  return result;
+}
+
+const ERR = "I couldn't find the answer this time due to an internal error in code generation, you can try again later.";
+
+console.log('\n[1] detector: the canned backend error matches; ordinary/long answers do not');
+ok(isTransientBackendError(ERR), 'the Gemini canned error matches');
+ok(isTransientBackendError('Internal error in code generation. Please try again later.'), 'a short variant matches');
+ok(!isTransientBackendError('Here is the answer: the app registers at /apps and the build compiled fine.'), 'a normal answer does not match');
+ok(!isTransientBackendError('I traced an internal error in code generation inside DevServerMain that you should fix; here is the full analysis of every method and how the classpath is assembled '.padEnd(500, '.')), 'a LONG answer discussing a code-gen error does not match (length guard)');
+ok(!isTransientBackendError('javac will throw an internal error in code generation if the AST is malformed.'), 'a SHORT legit sentence with the phrase but no "try again" does not match (bug review #13)');
+ok(!isTransientBackendError(''), 'empty prose does not match');
+
+console.log('\n[2] worker: re-runs past the transient error and returns the REAL answer');
+{
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-bte-'));
+  try {
+    const result = await run(dir, [ERR, 'Registration attaches the app at /apps and starts ng serve — all good.']);
+    ok(/Registration attaches/.test(result.content), `retried past the error → real answer (got: "${result.content.slice(0, 45)}…")`);
+    ok(!/internal error in code generation/i.test(result.content), 'the final answer is NOT the backend error');
+    ok(result.stats.turns >= 2, `took an extra turn to retry (turns=${result.stats.turns})`);
+    ok(result.events.some((e) => e.type === 'retract-assistant'), 'a retract-assistant event was emitted so the UI hides the fumbled turn');
+  } finally { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+}
+
+console.log('\n[3b] bug #14: prevTurnHadTools survives a transient retry (a following EMPTY reply still nudges, not wraps up)');
+{
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-bte3b-'));
+  try {
+    await (await import('node:fs/promises')).writeFile(path.join(dir, 'f.txt'), 'data');
+    // turn1: a tool call → turn2: transient error (retract+re-run, must NOT clobber prevTurnHadTools) →
+    // turn2': EMPTY reply → nudge (c) "read the results" should fire (because turn1 had tools) → turn3: real answer.
+    const result = await run(dir, [
+      '<remote-workspace><read path="f.txt"/></remote-workspace>',
+      ERR,
+      '',
+      'Here is the answer after re-reading the results.',
+    ]);
+    ok(/answer after re-reading/.test(result.content), `an empty reply after a transient retry still triggers nudge (c) → real answer (got: "${result.content.slice(0, 45)}…")`);
+    ok(!/don'?t have a written reply|without a written reply/i.test(result.content), 'it did NOT fall through to the generic no-reply closer (which is what the clobbered-state bug caused)');
+  } finally { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+}
+
+console.log('\n[3] worker: a PERSISTENT backend error eventually surfaces (bounded retries)');
+{
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-bte2-'));
+  try {
+    const result = await run(dir, [ERR]);   // every turn returns the error
+    ok(/internal error in code generation/i.test(result.content), 'after the retry budget, the error is shown (does not loop forever)');
+  } finally { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-behaviour-ladder.mjs
new a55883959692e078c2cf7fc5
+// The behaviour ladder, end to end through the HTTP endpoint, with injected fake adapters — no tokens, no network.
+// Three fakes, each pinning a property the ladder's usefulness depends on:
+//
+//   DECENT      behaves reasonably → the report has a score, a rung line per tier, and a problems section.
+//   MUTE        returns empty content → the FOUNDATION rungs must fail and the report must say the rungs above are
+//               unsafe to read. This is the gemini-3.1-pro-preview case: 0% everywhere because it never spoke.
+//               Nothing above tier 0 is a memory finding when the model did not answer.
+//   FABRICATOR  answers everything confidently, including a ticket it was never given → the CONTROL rung must
+//               catch it and the report must say the scorer is not measuring what it claims.
+//   AMNESIAC    sees ONLY the last message — the shape a live production run showed, where ~25 memory rungs failed
+//               and the model said "not mentioned in any of the visible messages". The ladder must report that as
+//               ONE delivery break with the memory rungs collapsed under it, not as 25 independent findings.
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { buildLadder, buildLadderContext, LADDER_TIERS } from '../src/agent/behaviour-ladder.mjs';
+import { probeToolAsks } from '../src/agent/model-probe.mjs';
+import { buildStaticPrompt, buildLiveTail } from '../src/agent/worker-prompt.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, label) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${label}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${label}\n`); } };
+
+const PLANTS = { file: 'src/ORANGE-WIDGET-7734.ts', env: 'ITB-3', flag: 'tampTyaCountryOverride', ticket: 'VBA-4471' };
+const VERBS = 'list read search find info write edit copy move mkdir remove unzip js bg-status wait-background kill-background git_clone author-plugins';
+
+const MARKER_RE = /MK-[A-Z]{3}\d+/g;
+
+function fake(mode) {
+  return {
+    async complete({ messages, max_tokens }) {
+      // AMNESIAC reads only the final message — everything the caller sent before it is invisible, which is exactly
+      // what a dropped-history transport looks like from inside the scorer.
+      const visible = mode === 'amnesiac' ? [messages[messages.length - 1]] : messages;
+      const all = visible.map((m) => m.content).join('\n');
+      if (mode === 'mute') return { choices: [{ message: { content: '' }, finish_reason: 'length' }] };
+      // Visibility rungs: answer honestly from whatever is actually visible.
+      if (/List EVERY marker token/.test(all)) return { choices: [{ message: { content: (all.match(MARKER_RE) || []).join(', ') || 'I see no markers.' } }] };
+      if (/marker did I give you in checkpoint 1/.test(all)) return { choices: [{ message: { content: (all.match(MARKER_RE) || [])[0] || 'I was not given one.' } }] };
+      if (/How many messages have I sent you/.test(all)) return { choices: [{ message: { content: String(visible.filter((m) => m.role === 'user').length) } }] };
+      // Whether this is a COMPACTION FOLD is decided on what was SENT, never on what this fake can see. Keying it on
+      // `all` made the amnesiac take the fold branch — its one visible message never contains the static prompt —
+      // and answer every memory rung with the full plant list, so a model seeing nothing scored as remembering
+      // everything and the mode under test measured its own opposite.
+      const allSent = messages.map((m) => m.content).join('\n');
+      if (!/You are code_boss/.test(allSent) && !/Reply with|Write one plain|exactly three words/.test(allSent)) {
+        return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'BRIEF: ' + Object.values(PLANTS).join(' ') } }] };   // a compaction fold
+      }
+      // Echo whichever literal the rung asked for, so tiers 0-1 mostly pass and the interesting rungs are reachable.
+      const literals = ['READY', 'BANANA', 'ALPHA-9', 'hello-world-42', 'aBcDeF'].filter((w) => all.includes(w));
+      const parts = [...literals];
+      if (/at least 60 words/.test(all)) parts.push(Array.from({ length: 70 }, (_, i) => 'word' + i).join(' '));
+      // The RUNG's exact ask, not the phrase: the prompt under test mentions "two or three words" (naming a
+      // sub-agent), and a loose match had this fake answering every protocol rung with "one two three".
+      if (/exactly three words/.test(all)) return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'one two three' } }] };
+      if (/characters unchanged|exactly this one line/.test(all)) parts.push('if (a < b && c > d) { x++; }');
+      if (/command verb/.test(all)) parts.push('Verbs: ' + VERBS);
+      if (/Read src\/config\.ts|read the file we discussed/.test(all)) parts.push('<remote-workspace><read path="src/config.ts"/></remote-workspace>');
+      if (/Delete the file/.test(all)) parts.push('<remote-workspace><remove path="src/obsolete.ts"/></remote-workspace>');
+      if (/fix the bug now/.test(all)) parts.push('I am in read-only mode, so I will only describe the change.');
+      if (/maven build|read files in this project/.test(all)) parts.push('Yes, I can do that myself.');
+      // Memory: only values genuinely present — except FABRICATOR, which answers everything.
+      for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(PLANTS)) {
+        if (mode === 'fabricator' || all.includes(v)) parts.push(v);
+      }
+      if (mode !== 'fabricator' && /DATABASE|what ticket number did I give/.test(all) && !all.includes(PLANTS.ticket)) {
+        return { choices: [{ message: { content: "You never mentioned that — it is not in the conversation." } }] };
+      }
+      if (/EARLIEST message/.test(all)) parts.push('the bug I want fixed is in ' + PLANTS.file);
+      if (/Can you see the earlier messages|earlier part of this conversation/.test(all)) parts.push('Yes, I can see them.');
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: parts.join(' \n') || 'ok' }, finish_reason: 'stop' }] };
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+const html = await readFile(new URL('../src/ui.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
+async function ladderVia(mode, port) {
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:1/v1', apiKey: 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' },
+    defaultModel: mode + '-model', port, modelsList: [{ id: mode + '-model' }], adapter: fake(mode), apiFormat: 'anthropic',
+  });
+  try {
+    const start = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/api/probe-model`, {
+      method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ model: mode + '-model', preset: 'ladder' }),
+    })).json();
+    if (!start?.ok) throw new Error('did not start: ' + JSON.stringify(start));
+    let s = null;
+    let misses = 0;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 600; i++) {
+      try {
+        s = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/api/probe-model?id=${start.id}`)).json();
+      } catch (e) {
+        if (++misses > 20) throw e;                                  // the server is genuinely gone; say so
+        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 200));
+        continue;
+      }
+      if (s.done) break;
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+    }
+    return s;
+  } finally { server.close(); }
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[1] the ladder itself\n');
+{
+  const prompts = { staticPrompt: buildStaticPrompt({ discussMode: false }), staticPromptDiscuss: buildStaticPrompt({ discussMode: true }),
+    liveTail: buildLiveTail({ projectDir: 'D:/x', projectContext: '', pluginToolsDoc: '' }) };
+  const roster = probeToolAsks(prompts.liveTail).roster;
+  const ctx = await buildLadderContext({ adapter: fake('decent'), model: 'm', prompts, roster });
+  const tests = buildLadder(ctx);
+  ok(tests.length === 56, `56 rungs (${tests.length})`);
+  ok(tests.every((t, i) => t.id === i + 1), 'rung ids are 1..56 in order');
+  ok(new Set(tests.map((t) => t.name)).size === tests.length, 'every rung has a distinct name');
+  const tiers = [...new Set(tests.map((t) => t.tier))];
+  ok(tiers.length === LADDER_TIERS.length && tiers.every((t, i) => t === i), 'rungs are ordered by tier, simplest first');
+  // The ladder only means anything if the bottom is genuinely simpler than the top.
+  ok(tests[0].messages && tests[0].messages.length === 1, 'rung 1 is a single bare message — no conversation, no prompt');
+  ok(tests.filter((t) => t.control).length >= 2, 'the scorer keeps its own controls as rungs');
+  ok(tests.filter((t) => !t.messages && !t.messagesFrom).length >= 4, 'fixture-only rungs exist (compaction is inspected, not re-asked)');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[1b] the two <js> API rungs, scored against what production actually emitted\n');
+{
+  const prompts = { staticPrompt: buildStaticPrompt({ discussMode: false }), staticPromptDiscuss: buildStaticPrompt({ discussMode: true }),
+    liveTail: buildLiveTail({ projectDir: 'D:/x', projectContext: '', pluginToolsDoc: '' }) };
+  const roster = probeToolAsks(prompts.liveTail).roster;
+  const ctx = await buildLadderContext({ adapter: fake('decent'), model: 'm', prompts, roster });
+  const rung = (name) => buildLadder(ctx).find((t) => t.name === name);
+  const block = (body) => `Sure.\n<remote-workspace>\n<js>${body}</js>\n</remote-workspace>`;
+  const jsOn = roster.includes('js');
+
+  const reach = rung('js-no-node-reach');
+  ok(!!reach, 'the fs-reach rung exists');
+  if (jsOn) {
+    // Verbatim shape from the live gemini-3.1-pro run.
+    const live = reach.check(block('const fs = require("fs");\nconst files = fs.readdirSync("portlet-pcm");\nreturn files.length;'));
+    ok(live.pass === false && /require\(\)/.test(live.note), 'the exact live failure (require("fs") + readdirSync) is caught and named');
+    ok(reach.check(block('const e = await list("portlet-pcm");\nreturn e.filter((x) => x.name.endsWith(".java")).length;')).pass === true,
+      'the list() answer the sandbox now supports passes');
+    ok(reach.check(block('const e = await search({name:"portlet-pcm/*.java"}); return e.length;')).pass === true, 'a search() answer passes too — the rung tests the API reach, not one blessed call');
+    ok(reach.check(block('const p = path.join("a","b"); return p;')).pass === false, 'reaching for node:path is caught as well');
+    ok(reach.check('I would list the directory and count the .java files.').pass === false, 'prose with no <js> block does not pass by default');
+  } else ok(reach.check('').pass === null, 'with <js> off the rung reports NOT MEASURED rather than a false failure');
+
+  const sub = rung('js-runs-in-subdir');
+  ok(!!sub, 'the subdirectory rung exists');
+  if (jsOn) {
+    // The live shape: a bare string in the option slot. It is now READ as dir, so it must pass — the rung
+    // measures whether the command gets scoped at all, not whether it was spelled the long way.
+    ok(sub.check(block('const out = await git(["grep","-i","distance"], "portlet-pcm"); return out.stdout;')).pass === true,
+      'the live bare-string form passes now that a string is read as dir');
+    ok(sub.check(block('const out = await git(["grep","-i","distance"], { dir: "portlet-pcm" }); return out.stdout;')).pass === true, 'the canonical {dir} form passes');
+    ok(sub.check(block('const out = await git(["grep","-i","distance"], { cwd: "portlet-pcm" }); return out.stdout;')).pass === true, '{cwd} passes — it is an accepted alias');
+    const shell = sub.check(block('const out = await git("grep -i distance", { dir: "portlet-pcm" }); return out.stdout;'));
+    ok(shell.pass === false && /argv ARRAY/.test(shell.note), 'a shell string instead of an argv array fails, and the note says which');
+    const cd = sub.check(block('await git(["cd","portlet-pcm"]);\nconst out = await git(["grep","-i","distance"]); return out.stdout;'));
+    ok(cd.pass === false && /no cd/.test(cd.note), 'trying to cd fails with the reason');
+    ok(sub.check(block('const out = await git(["grep","-i","distance"]); return out.stdout;')).pass === false, 'never scoping the command to the subdirectory fails');
+  } else ok(sub.check('').pass === null, 'with <js> off the subdirectory rung is NOT MEASURED');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[2] a DECENT model — report shape\n');
+{
+  const s = await ladderVia('decent', 18961);
+  const rep = String(s?.report || '');
+  ok(s?.done && !s?.error, 'run completed');
+  ok(/^code_boss behaviour ladder · decent-model/m.test(rep), 'header names the model under test');
+  ok(/^SCORE \d+\/\d+ \(\d+%\)/m.test(rep), 'an overall score is the first thing after the header');
+  ok(/holds through "/.test(rep), 'it says how far up the ladder the model held');
+  for (const t of LADDER_TIERS) ok(rep.includes(t.name), `tier "${t.name}" appears in the inline results`);
+  ok(/\d+✓/.test(rep) && /CONFIRMED PROBLEMS/.test(rep), 'rungs are listed inline and problems are called out below');
+  ok(Math.max(...rep.split('\n').map((l) => l.length)) <= 92, `no line exceeds 92 columns (max ${Math.max(...rep.split('\n').map((l) => l.length))})`);
+  ok(rep.split('\n').length <= 48, `fits a screenshot (${rep.split('\n').length} lines)`);
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[3] a MUTE model — the foundation must fail, loudly\n');
+{
+  const s = await ladderVia('mute', 18962);
+  const rep = String(s?.report || '');
+  ok(/FOUNDATION FAILED/.test(rep), 'the report leads with a foundation failure rather than a memory verdict');
+  ok(/unsafe to read/.test(rep), 'it says the rungs above cannot be trusted');
+  ok(/#1\s+responds/.test(rep) || /\b1✗/.test(rep), 'rung 1 (does it answer at all) is marked failed');
+  ok(/returned no content/.test(rep), 'the problem is stated as observed behaviour, not inferred');
+  const score = (rep.match(/SCORE (\d+)\//) || [])[1];
+  ok(Number(score) <= 2, `a mute model scores near zero (${score}) — silence must not pass negative-assertion rungs`);
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[4] a FABRICATOR — the control rung must catch it\n');
+{
+  const s = await ladderVia('fabricator', 18963);
+  const rep = String(s?.report || '');
+  ok(/CONTROLS FAILED/.test(rep), 'inventing a fact it was never told trips the control rung');
+  ok(/no-fabrication/.test(rep), 'the failing control is named');
+  ok(/invented a ticket number/.test(rep), 'the problem line quotes what it actually did');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[5] an AMNESIAC — one delivery break, not 25 memory findings\n');
+{
+  const s = await ladderVia('amnesiac', 18964);
+  const rep = String(s?.report || '');
+  ok(/NOT REACHING THE MODEL/.test(rep), 'the report leads with the delivery break, not with a memory verdict');
+  ok(/visible-turns-bare/.test(rep), 'the rung that measured it is named');
+  ok(/saw NONE of the 10 markers|saw \d+\/10 markers/.test(rep), 'it reports HOW MANY turns arrived, as an observation');
+  // Bare and wrapped both fail here, so the blame lands on the transport rather than on assembleConvo. Getting this
+  // backwards would send someone hunting through our prompt assembly for a fault that is not in it.
+  ok(/endpoint or the model itself/.test(rep) && /NOT our prompt assembly/i.test(rep),
+    'blame is attributed to the transport, since even a BARE conversation lost turns');
+  ok(/memory\/recall rungs failed/.test(rep) && /downstream of the break above/.test(rep),
+    'the memory rungs are collapsed to a count under the root cause');
+  // The delivery break must OUTRANK the control banner: controls that need history cannot pass when history is not
+  // arriving, and "the scorer is broken" would send the reader hunting in the wrong place.
+  ok(!/CONTROLS FAILED/.test(rep), 'a control that depends on history is not reported as a broken scorer');
+  const listed = (rep.match(/^ #\d+ /gm) || []).length;
+  ok(listed <= 12, `the problem list stays readable (${listed} individually listed)`);
+  ok(rep.split('\n').length <= 48, `still fits a screenshot when everything fails (${rep.split('\n').length} lines)`);
+  // Collapsing is a DISPLAY decision, not a way to quietly drop failures: the headline count must still equal every
+  // rung that failed, listed plus collapsed.
+  const total = Number((rep.match(/CONFIRMED PROBLEMS\s+\((\d+)\)/) || [])[1]);
+  const collapsed = Number((rep.match(/\+(\d+) memory\/recall rungs failed/) || [])[1]);
+  ok(total === listed + collapsed, `every failure is still counted: ${listed} listed + ${collapsed} collapsed = ${total}`);
+  // THIS USED TO ASSERT >= 10, and the drop to 4 is the flat layout working rather than a regression.
+  //
+  // The amnesiac mock reads ONLY the last message of what it is sent — which is api.genai.mil, measured. Under
+  // the old multi-message array it therefore saw one turn of ten and seventeen memory rungs failed downstream.
+  // assembleConvo now sends ONE message containing the whole conversation, so the message it does read carries
+  // everything, and most of those rungs simply pass. The ladder's own simulation of the problem now shows the
+  // change defeating it.
+  //
+  // The collapse still has to WORK — a real delivery break must not print as twenty separate findings — so this
+  // keeps a floor, at the level an amnesiac can still reach: the rungs that need turns to arrive AS TURNS.
+  ok(collapsed >= 3, `the collapse still does real work (${collapsed} rungs folded into one line)`);
+  ok(total < 15, `and an amnesiac transport no longer cascades into a wall of memory findings (${total}) — the conversation rides in the one message it reads`);
+}
+
+process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+
=== end
